#### Hidden: Rust unit tests

| Test | Rust unit test |
| --- | --- |
| `merge-hold.test.ts:119` merge hold memo › answers an unchanged stamp from the last derivation, spawning no git | merge hold memo: a second derivation with an unchanged ref stamp and the same done candidates returns the last held list and runs no git command. |
| `merge-hold.test.ts:129` merge hold memo › derives again when the stamp moves, so a merge that landed is seen | merge hold memo: a moved ref stamp forces a fresh derivation, so a merge that landed since releases the Ticket instead of a stale held answer being served. |
| `merge-hold.test.ts:141` merge hold memo › derives again when another ticket reaches done, whatever the stamp says | merge hold memo: another Ticket reaching done changes the memo key and forces a fresh derivation even when the ref stamp has not moved. |
| `merge-hold.test.ts:149` merge hold memo › derives every time a probe cannot vouch for its refs | merge hold memo: when the probe cannot vouch for its refs (no stamp, or a ref file written inside the racy window) the hold is derived with git on every call. |
| `merge-hold.test.ts:309` merge hold watch › runs nothing while the emitted set is empty | merge hold watch: while the last emitted hold set is empty the watch runs no derivation at all. |
| `merge-hold.test.ts:361` merge hold watch › stops for good once stopped, whatever is emitted afterwards | merge hold watch: once a dead or closed drive stops it, the watch never derives again, whatever hold set is reported afterwards. |
| `merge-hold.test.ts:418` wait-and-recompute rule › hands back the first recompute when nothing holds, without a wait | wait-and-recompute rule: a first recompute that finds nothing held is handed back at once, with no hold derivation, no wait and no emit. |
| `merge-hold.test.ts:431` wait-and-recompute rule › re-waits a hold that re-engages between the wait and the recompute, never handing back a held set | wait-and-recompute rule: a hold that re-engages between a wait's exit and the next recompute starts a second wait, logged again, so a value computed under a hold is never handed back. |

#### Dropped

| Test | Why it is dropped |
| --- | --- |
| `merge-hold.test.ts:158` merge hold memo › hands every caller its own list | drop: checks that the memoised hold hands each caller its own array so a caller mutating it cannot corrupt the cache, a JavaScript aliasing concern Rust ownership rules out. |

<a id="spawns"></a>

### `spawns`: Spawns

Spawn proposals in Outcomes (schema, ids, blocks, requested Assignment), adoption at the boundary, namespace reservation, Pending and Held spawns, Spawn caps, the Adopt, Hold and Discard routes, what the Spawn ledger lists and when it changes.

68 visible, 1 hidden, 0 dropped.

#### Visible: conformance cases

| Test | Conformance case |
| --- | --- |

| `engine.test.ts:8013` outcome spawn schema › keeps an empty spawn array without rejections | Pool with Ticket 01 and a stub whose done Outcome carries spawn []; let it exit; /api/state state.outcomes['01'].spawn is an empty list and runs/01.events.jsonl holds no spawn-rejected event. |
| `engine.test.ts:8022` outcome spawn schema › rejects a spawn key that is not an array | Pool with Ticket 01 and a stub whose done Outcome carries spawn 'yes'; let it exit; 01 still ends done, state.outcomes['01'].spawn is an empty list, and runs/01.events.jsonl holds one spawn-rejected event with reason 'spawn is not an array' and no index. |
| `engine.test.ts:8031` outcome spawn schema › rejects a malformed spawn entry per proposal with a clear reason | Pool with Ticket 01 whose done Outcome carries one malformed proposal, for each of 12 shapes (title missing or blank, body missing, blank or under 20 characters, blockedBy not a list of non-blank strings, entry not an object); let it exit; spawn-rejected carries index 0 and the matching reason, and state.outcomes['01'].spawn is empty. |

| `engine.test.ts:9654` pending spawns (issue #150) › lands a spawn queued while the drive was closing, without another kick | drive close: an Adopt of a Held spawn accepted while the drive is closing (after its last boundary, as the Review is raised) keeps the run quiescent and starts a fresh drive that lands the spawn and runs it to done without another kick. |

<a id="attempts"></a>

### `attempts`: Attempts and harness launch

Launch, run and end of an Attempt, headless or terminal-backed: harness descriptors, argv, env, model and effort, the ticket prompt and its outcome and spawn instructions, the wrapper and readiness and paste echo checks, headless fallbacks when herdr refuses, the Stream file and the derived attempt log, Attempt endings (exit codes, herdr pane ends, liveness) and exit facts, Botched launches, Folder trust and Blocking dialogs, live attempts.

177 visible, 12 hidden, 8 dropped.

#### Visible: conformance cases

| Test | Conformance case |
| --- | --- |
| `attempt-ending.test.ts:62` waitForPaneEnding › ends on the pane's own end, without waiting on a file | Terminal-backed pool, fake rendering claude's ready frame, stub claude that holds without an Outcome; once the prompt is typed the fake ends the pane and no exit-code file is written; the crash event lands within seconds, not after a liveness sweep, its reason naming the unreadable runs/01.exitcode. |
| `attempt-ending.test.ts:80` waitForPaneEnding › ends on the exit-code file while the subscription is healthy and silent | Terminal-backed pool whose fake keeps a pane's shell after its command exits and sends no pane event, stub claude that exits 3 a few seconds after its prompt with no Outcome; let it run; the attempt ends on runs/01.exitcode (exited code 3, crash 'harness exited 3') and its events.subscribe connection is closed. *Seam: fake herdr reports open subscriber connections.* |
| `attempt-ending.test.ts:101` waitForPaneEnding › ends on the exit-code file after the daemon hangs up on the subscriber | Terminal-backed pool whose fake drops every events.subscribe connection, stub claude that exits 0 with no Outcome after its prompt lands; let it run; runs/01.events.jsonl gets exited code 0 and crash 'no outcome written' within seconds, the attempt ended on runs/01.exitcode with no pane event. |
| `attempt-ending.test.ts:120` waitForPaneEnding › stays waiting after hang-up while the pane is still listed and no file appears | Terminal-backed pool whose fake drops every subscription, stub claude that holds without an Outcome; let several pane.list sweeps pass with the pane still listed; GET /api/state still shows Ticket 01 running with its Live attempt and no crash event, and once the stub exits the attempt ends on runs/01.exitcode. *Seam: short liveness and grace cadences (30 s and 10 s otherwise).* |
| `attempt-ending.test.ts:150` waitForPaneEnding › ends as a crash when the pane is gone and no file follows it | Terminal-backed pool whose fake drops every subscription, stub claude holding without an Outcome; the fake ends the pane after the prompt, so no event or exit-code file follows; after a liveness sweep and the grace window the crash reason is 'harness pane gone: &lt;pane&gt; left herdr's listing and no exit code was written to &lt;runs/01.exitcode&gt;'. *Seam: short liveness and grace cadences (30 s and 10 s otherwise).* |
| `attempt-ending.test.ts:167` waitForPaneEnding › records the real exit code when the file lands inside the grace window | Terminal-backed pool whose fake drops subscriptions and delists Ticket 01's pane while its process runs on; the stub exits 7 inside the grace window, writing runs/01.exitcode; the attempt records exited code 7 and crash 'harness exited 7', not a pane-gone crash. *Seam: fake delists a pane without ending its process; short cadences.* |
| `attempt-ending.test.ts:195` waitForPaneEnding › leaves a healthy long attempt waiting however many sweeps pass | Terminal-backed pool, stub claude that holds a long time without an Outcome; let many pane.list sweeps pass with the pane listed; no exited or crash event appears and GET /api/state shows Ticket 01 running throughout, until the stub exits and the attempt ends on its exit code. *Seam: short liveness and grace cadences (30 s and 10 s otherwise).* |
| `attempt-ending.test.ts:223` waitForPaneEnding › rides out a daemon that cannot answer for the pane at all | Terminal-backed pool whose fake answers every pane.list with an error, stub claude that holds without an Outcome; let several sweeps be refused; no crash is recorded and Ticket 01 stays running until the stub exits, when the attempt ends on runs/01.exitcode with the stub's own code. *Seam: short liveness and grace cadences (30 s and 10 s otherwise).* |
| `attempt-ending.test.ts:318` waitForAttemptEnding with a headless watch › ends on the child's exit and reads the result after | Headless pool, stub claude that writes a done Outcome and exits 0; let Ticket 01 run; runs/01.events.jsonl holds spawned then exited with code 0, status done and outcomeExists true, and no crash event. |
| `attempt-ending.test.ts:335` waitForAttemptEnding with a headless watch › carries the child's own code and names the harness on a crash | Headless pool, stub claude that exits 3 without an Outcome; let Ticket 01 run; the exited event carries code 3 and outcomeExists false, and both the crash event and the crash Interrupt body give the reason 'harness exited 3'. |
| `attempt-ending.test.ts:349` waitForAttemptEnding with a headless watch › reads a clean exit with no result as the missing-outcome crash | Headless pool, stub claude that exits 0 without writing an Outcome; let Ticket 01 run; the exited event has code 0, and the crash event and crash Interrupt give the reason 'no outcome written'. |
| `attempt-ending.test.ts:429` exitCrashReason › names the harness and its code for a real exit | Pool whose stub claude exits 3 without an Outcome; let Ticket 01 run; the crash event's reason is 'harness exited 3' and the crash Interrupt body opens 'crash: harness exited 3'. |
| `attempt-ending.test.ts:435` exitCrashReason › carries the subject through, so a resolver reads as one | Pool with a merge conflict on Ticket 01 and console.json resolver claude, whose stub exits 2 when run as the resolver; let the resolver Attempt run; the merge-conflict Interrupt body ends 'The resolver agent attempted: resolver exited 2'. |
| `attempt-ending.test.ts:441` exitCrashReason › points at the unwritten file when no code arrived | Terminal-backed pool, stub claude holding without an Outcome; the fake ends the pane so the wrapper never writes runs/01.exitcode; the crash reason reads 'harness exit code unreadable: the pane wrapper never wrote a usable &lt;runs/01.exitcode&gt;' and never 'exited -1'. |

| `attempt-ending.test.ts:474` exitedPhrase › reads as the shell's own status for a real exit | Pool with Ticket 01's stub exiting 3 without an Outcome and Ticket 02's exiting 0 with a done one; let both run; GET /api/pool-log holds 'ticket 01: exited 3, marker in-progress, crash: harness exited 3' and 'ticket 02: exited 0, marker done'. |

| Test | Rust unit test |
| --- | --- |
| `attempt-ending.test.ts:269` waitForPaneEnding › ends when the caller releases it | pane ending wait: a release from its caller (a Conversation ended by the operator) settles the wait as released, never as an Attempt ending, and drops its herdr subscription. |
| `attempt-run.test.ts:435` headless attempts › throws the pool config error for an assignment the harness table cannot serve | attempt launch guard: an Assignment with no harness or no model, or naming a harness the harness table lacks, is refused with the pool config error naming the Ticket ('has no harness', 'names unknown harness ...') and launches nothing. |
| `claude-trust.test.ts:122` seedClaudeFolderTrust (issue #127) › seeds the realpath beside the path when the two differ | folder trust seed: when the directory's path and its realpath differ, both get a trusted entry, since claude keys its map by the kernel-resolved cwd (the engine itself always hands a canonical worktree path). |
| `engine.test.ts:6625` terminal-backed engine mechanics (ADR-0014) › the darwin resize relay execs the harness with its exit code, and starts no loop without a terminal (issue #136) | darwin resize relay: run under sh with a pane terminal name that is not a tty, it starts no resize loop, execs the harness with its argv intact (words holding a space and a quote) and exits with the harness's own code (7). |
| `engine.test.ts:6651` terminal-backed engine mechanics (ADR-0014) › the darwin resize relay copies each pane resize onto the harness's PTY and signals it (issue #136) (`skipIf`) | darwin resize relay: given a real pane PTY, each resize of the pane (30x100, then 40x120, then 25x70) is copied onto the harness's own PTY size and the harness receives SIGWINCH for each. |
| `herdr.test.ts:376` waitForPaneEnd › settles lost at once when released mid-connect, and still lets go of the socket | pane end watch: released while its subscription connect is still in flight, it resolves lost at once and closes the connection once the connect completes, leaving no subscriber on the daemon. |
| `herdr.test.ts:390` waitForPaneEnd › resolves lost without connecting when released before it starts | pane end watch: a release signalled before the watch starts resolves lost without opening any connection to the daemon. |
| `live-attempts.test.ts:39` live attempts registry › leaves out the ids the caller excludes, a Conversation's pane riding its own view | live attempt records: the per-Ticket view leaves out ids the caller excludes (a live Conversation's, whose pane rides its own view), while that id still counts as live. |
| `live-attempts.test.ts:70` live attempts registry › notifies on every change and stays quiet on a clear that changes nothing | live attempt registry: a register or a clear that changes the live set notifies its owner once (the owner pushes a snapshot), and a clear of an attempt never registered notifies nothing. |
| `spawn.test.ts:244` defaultHarnessDescriptors › exposes one canonical readiness pattern fixture per harness | harness table: every known harness (claude, opencode, cursor) declares a non-empty ready pattern, so readiness can never match an empty pane. |
| `spawn.test.ts:250` defaultHarnessDescriptors › treats an unknown harness as a raw descriptor-shaped gap, exactly as the mode lookup does | harness table: a harness name with no descriptor yields no descriptor and the raw stream mode, the same answer the stream-mode lookup gives. |
| `spawn.test.ts:384` elidePromptArgv › elides every element that carries the body and passes an argv with none through unchanged | spawned event argv elision: every argv element containing the prompt body gets the body replaced by &lt;prompt&gt;, elements without it pass unchanged, and an empty body elides nothing. |

<a id="herdr"></a>

### `herdr`: herdr panes

The pool's herdr panes outside an Attempt's launch and ending: the RPC client, the Pool workspace, tab labels and tab closing, pane survey and liveness, Peek and pane reads, focus, Held panes, Finished terminals, the terminal routes and the liveAttempt pane on the snapshot.

88 visible, 2 hidden, 1 dropped.

#### Visible: conformance cases

| --- | --- |

<a id="conversations"></a>

### `conversations`: Conversations

Conversation storage, start, launch and ending, Turn state, Notices to a parent and their texts, a Conversation's spawn adoption, the conversation routes, Seeded Pools.

90 visible, 4 hidden, 0 dropped.

<a id="steward"></a>

### `steward`: Steward

The Steward role: what its `console.json` entry does (Steward budget, may Close), what it is told (Notices, stalled Merge queue head), its answers and their limits, Steward notes, its routes, its teaching text. The steward command line is `cli`.

37 visible, 0 hidden, 0 dropped.

#### Visible: conformance cases

| Test | Conformance case |
| --- | --- |
| `prompt.test.ts:383` buildStewardTeaching (ADR-0030) › names the skill, the exact command and every verb, and the budget in force | Terminal-backed pool with console.json steward.budget 1; POST /api/conversations with role steward; the teaching typed into its pane says to load my-console-steward, gives the steward command with --pool and --as &lt;its id&gt;, every verb (answer, keep-talking, leave, held, reassign, state, end), and a budget of 1 answer per Ticket. |
| `prompt.test.ts:397` buildStewardTeaching (ADR-0030) › lists close only while Steward may Close is on, with its rule | Stewards started in pools with console.json steward.mayClose off and on; read the teachings typed into their panes; the first lists no close verb and says Closing is the operator's, the second lists close &lt;ticket&gt; &lt;note&gt; with its rule and budget cost, both saying state tells whether Close is allowed now. |
| `prompt.test.ts:411` buildStewardTeaching (ADR-0030) › states the exclusions, the hands-off rule, the push rule, and how to leave and end | Terminal-backed pool; POST /api/conversations with role steward; the teaching says never to answer a review or persistence Interrupt, to make no edits in any worktree or the pool checkout, to push only if the operator's words allow, to leave with a recommending note, and to end with a closing line. |

<a id="verify"></a>

### `verify`: Verify and Jev

`verify: N` fan-out, grader Tickets, Jev grading (Evidence, rubric, fallbacks), Selection and head-to-head, human selection, Adopt a Candidate, the grades endpoint and the Winner, Jev at boot.

85 visible, 20 hidden, 5 dropped.

#### Visible: conformance cases

| Test | Conformance case |
| --- | --- |
| `engine.test.ts:607` verify assignment › runs a pool with no verify key exactly as today | Pool with Ticket 01 and console.json defaults only, no verify key; run the pool and approve Review; the phase is done, stub claude launched exactly once for 01, and no grader or head-to-head Ticket file exists in issues/. |
| `engine.test.ts:711` verify fan-out › fans a verify: 3 ticket out to three same-round attempts, each on its own branch | Git pool, Ticket 01 at verify 3, stub claude attempts that commit a file and wait for a sibling; run the pool; the three run at once on pool/&lt;key&gt;/01.attempt-N branches from one HEAD, all scheduled before any spawned, then three graders and 01-head-to-head, attempt 1 merges and every attempt branch is deleted. |
| `engine.test.ts:856` verify fan-out › marks a verify: 1 ticket done on a passing grade, merging its attempt branch | Git pool with Ticket 01 at verify 1 whose attempt commits cand.txt and whose grader passes; run the pool and approve Review; 01 reads status=done, cand.txt is merged, the attempt branch is deleted, and runs/01.events.jsonl reads scheduled, spawned, exited, graded, merged. |
| `engine.test.ts:890` verify fan-out › runs a ticket without verify exactly one attempt, as today, beside a fan-out | Git pool with Ticket 01 at verify 3 and Ticket 02 with no verify key, each committing work; run the pool; 02 launches once with events scheduled, spawned, exited, merged, while 01 launches three attempts, merges attempt 1's work and deletes all three attempt branches. |
